Argument Breakdown under the stimulus\n261:\n262:- New `src/components/drill/official/ArgumentBreakdown.tsx`, props\n263:  `{ data, isLoading, isError, activeStyle, onStyleChange, fontScale }`.\n264:  A `border-t` rule, the style pills only when `styles.length > 1`, then\n265:  labelled steps (label on its own line, slate-700 600-weight ~0.85 × base;\n266:  body sans at base size, 1.6 line-height).\n267:- Shared helper `resolveBreakdownSteps(data, activeStyle)` in\n268:  `useLrExplanation.ts` carries the existing borrow-Standard rule (a\n269:  non-Standard style with zero steps renders Standard\'s, labelled "same as\n270:  Standard"), moved out of `DrillExplanationTab` unchanged. The rule is\n271:  today\'s shipped behaviour and the label is honest about it; whether the\n272:  ingest recorded "(same as Standard)" literally is checked during the\n273:  build and noted in the helper\'s comment either way.\n274:- `OfficialQuestionPane` gets `belowStimulus?: React.ReactNode`. The slot is\n275:  a **sibling** of the stimulus `div`, inside a new left-column wrapper\n276:  `flex flex-col overflow-y-auto`; the stimulus `div` loses only its own\n277:  `overflow-y-auto` and keeps every other class: serif,\n278:  `whitespace-pre-wrap`, `[&>*]:max-w-[52ch]`, padding, highlight handlers.\n279:  The slot has its own `mt-6 px-6 sm:pl-9 sm:pr-8 font-sans\n280:  whitespace-normal max-w-[60ch]`.\n281:- `Drill.tsx` passes `belowStimulus={explanationOpen ? <ArgumentBreakdown …/> : null}`.\n282:- The drawer\'s Explanation tab stays, rendered from the same data.\n283:\n284:### 8. Choice Notes as dropdowns\n285:\n286:- `InlineChoiceExplanation` gains `open: boolean` and `onToggle: () => void`.\n287:  Header row (tag chip, "% of students", "Your answer") always renders when\n288:  any of the three exists. The chevron (`ChevronUp`/`ChevronDown`,\n289:  `aria-expanded`, "Hide explanation for A" / "Show explanation for A") and\n290:  the prose render **only when `note.trim().length > 0`**. A selected\n291:  choice with no published note gets its "Your answer" chip and no\n292:  dropdown; `isInlineChoiceEmpty` keeps its job.\n293:- The choice grid gains a 4th column so the chevron aligns under the\n294:  Check/X icon column (shared with the Restore button from step 3).\n295:- `OfficialQuestionPane` owns `openLetters: Set<string>`, reset to every\n296:  letter with a trimmed note whenever `inlineNotes` becomes non-null or the\n297:  qid changes (pressing Explanation always opens expanded, per the\n298:  recording). Turning Explanation off discards it.\n299:- The "ALWAYS VISIBLE" comment at the top of `InlineChoiceExplanation` is\n300:  rewritten to state the new rule and why.\n301:\n302:### 9. Tooltips on the pills\n303:\n304:`OfficialSessionStrip`: Answer `title` = "Show correct answer" / "Hide\n305:correct answer"; Explanation `title` = "Show explanations" / "Hide\n306:explanations". Copy from the LSAT Lab recording.\n307:\n308:### 10. Census of questions with no authored explanation\n309:\n310:- `scripts/missing-explanations-census.mjs`. Loads the service key the way\n311:  `scripts/db-pull.mjs` does, and **pages every table at 1000 rows with\n312:  `Range` headers until a short page**, the same loop `db-pull.mjs` uses.\n313:  Aborts (non-zero exit, nothing written) on any query error or if a table\'s\n314:  row count disagrees with a `count=exact` head request.\n315:- Reads the bank from `public/data/PT*-LR.json` (156 files).\n316:- **One predicate, two runtimes, one fixture set.** `hasAuthoredProse` (a\n317:  step or choice-note `body` non-empty after `trim()`) is implemented in\n318:  `useLrExplanation.ts` (replacing the row-count `hasAuthored`) and in the\n319:  script. A vitest spec `explanationProse.fixtures.test.ts` runs **both**\n320:  against the same fixtures (`src/lib/__fixtures__/explanationProse.json`:\n321:  empty, whitespace-only, null, mixed-style, note-only, step-only) by\n322:  importing the `.mjs` predicate directly, so they cannot drift.\n323:- Reasons are exactly what the data proves: `not ingested` (no\n324:  `lr_questions` row for the address) or `no authored prose` (row exists,\n325:  nothing passes the predicate). No "video only" label: nothing in the\n326:  schema records a video; the 14 known video-only questions (PT143, PT146\n327:  per the 2026-08-03 migration) are a subset of "no authored prose", noted\n328:  in the report header.\n329:- Output `ai-os/wiki/explanations/MISSING-EXPLANATIONS.md`: summary counts\n330:  (bank total, ingested, with prose, missing by reason), then one table per\n331:  PrepTest, rows `PT · S · Q · question type · reason`, sorted PT, section,\n332:  question. Run once, committed to the vault, not the app repo.\n333:\n334:### 11. Tests\n335:\n336:- `drillReviewPolicy.test.ts` (new): every launch kind maps to exactly one\n337:  policy; sealed and `ptRun` runs map to `none`.\n338:- `OfficialQuestionPane.test.tsx`: the four Answer × Explanation\n339:  combinations; Explanation on → every noted letter expanded; chevron folds\n340:  one; new qid re-expands; a selected choice with no note shows the chip\n341:  and no chevron; choices stay clickable while `answerRevealed` when\n342:  `choicesStayLive`; everything disabled under `interactionLocked`;\n343:  eliminated correct choice has a working Restore; Reset Response disabled\n344:  with no pick, clears the pick, leaves `answerRevealed` alone.\n345:- `InlineChoiceExplanation`: closed renders header, no prose; whitespace\n346:  note renders no chevron.\n347:- `tutorBehavior.test.ts` (new): `resolveTutorLaunch` with\n348:  `flagEnabled=false` never opens, whatever `tutorModeEnabled` is.\n349:- Drill-level, `fixed-list` (a guided drill): no Submit pill; pick →\n350:  attempt in session with trail; Answer with a pick flips, no dialog;\n351:  Answer with no pick → confirm, Cancel changes nothing; Explanation same;\n352:  after Answer on, a new pick lands in `practiceByQid` and the attempt is\n353:  unchanged; Reset then a new pick writes no attempt; End persists the\n354:  recorded attempts; **remount** restores the recorded attempt and oval,\n355:  drops the practice pick, and mutates no attempt, journal or Run Record;\n356:  no tutor after a wrong pick.\n357:- Drill-level, `fixed-commit` (a filter drill): arrow commits; failed save\n358:  leaves the question in place, writes nothing, emits one failure event;\n359:  double arrow and arrow-then-End each persist once and move once; pill\n360:  with an uncommitted pick commits first.\n361:- Drill-level, `adaptive`: reveal via pill on an unsubmitted pick marks\n362:  unscored and Submit is refused; after Submit the pick is frozen.\n363:- Drill-level, full section and a sealed assignment: both pills behave as\n364:  before.\n365:- Existing tests asserting the tutor opens after a wrong answer, or that a\n366:  fixed queue renders a Submit pill, are updated.\n367:\n368:### 12. Verify in the browser\n369:\n370:Preview server, at 1280, 1024 and 390 widths and with the drawer docked:\n371:- A guided drill: bottom bar is arrows + ovals, no Submit; pick B, press\n372:  Answer → B red, correct green, nothing else; Answer again → colours gone;\n373:  Explanation → breakdown under stimulus, dropdowns open; fold one;\n374:  Explanation off → dropdowns and breakdown gone; right arrow → oval marks\n375:  answered; back arrow → pick and states as left; Reset Response → pick\n376:  cleared, timer running; pick again → the recorded attempt in the session\n377:  map is unchanged and, after End, the `attempts` row count for that user +\n378:  qid grew by exactly one (the recorded attempt) relative to a count taken\n379:  before the drill; End Drill closes out.\n380:- A filter drill: same, plus arrow persists per question.\n381:- Smart Drill: its own bar unchanged (Submit, Skip, auto-reveal switch);\n382:  pills, breakdown, dropdowns and Reset work; no tutor after a wrong\n383:  Submit.\n384:- Cold cache first open: with an empty React Query cache, press Explanation\n385:  on a fresh question; the slot shows "Loading explanation…" then the\n386:  content, with no layout jump of the stimulus block.\n387:- A sealed run and a full section: selection and navigation unchanged.\n388:- Computed style of the stimulus `div` (font-family, font-size, padding,\n389:  max-width) identical before and after, read with `getComputedStyle`.\n390:- Screenshots for Joshua.\n391:\n392:## Key decisions & tradeoffs\n393:\n394:- **Classify by launch kind, not `mode`.** ADR 0016 says `mode` is not the\n395:  run kind; `isPracticeSetMode` is mutable state set mid-init. A synchronous\n396:  policy from the spec is the only thing that cannot misclassify a frame.\n397:- **Freeze on first reveal.** Before the answer is seen, today\'s pick\n398:  semantics stand (change of mind is recorded). After, the attempt is final\n399:  and everything is practice. Closes the reveal-then-switch hole in every\n400:  mode without locking the choices.\n401:- **One action lock, not per-qid pending state.** One question is on screen\n402:  at a time; a boolean that disables everything during a commit is simpler\n403:  and cannot re-enable early.\n404:- **Fixed drills keep their bar.** It already has the arrows and ovals; only\n405:  the Submit pill and the auto-reveal switch go.\n406:- **Flag gates mounting, not just state.** Round 1 showed the state-only\n407:  version leaked through the legacy controls.\n408:- **Answer and Explanation decoupled at the state level**, only in the\n409:  `reviewControlsAvailable` modes, with today\'s code as the else branch.\n410:- **Failures stop the caller chain and are reported structurally.**\n411:- **Pane changes are opt-in props.** `choicesStayLive`, `belowStimulus`,\n412:  `onResetResponse`, `interactionLocked` all default to today\'s behaviour.\n413:- **Dropdowns open by default, chevron only with prose.**\n414:- **Census predicate is fixture-tested in both runtimes.**\n415:- **Flag, not deletion** of the tutor. Joshua wants it back later.\n416:\n417:## Risks / open questions\n418:\n419:- `showSolution` / `showReviewButton` remain referenced by the legacy\n420:  layout; the audit may find the official surface still reads them.\n421:- Breakdown label sizing vs LSAT Lab is a taste call; verify on screen.\n422:- The census needs the service key from `~/ai-os/.env`; if absent it says\n423:  so and exits non-zero.\n424:\n425:## Out of scope\n426:\n427:- Smart Drill\'s question selection bug and its bottom bar.\n428:- A server-side kill switch on the `tutor-chat` edge function. Edge\n429:  functions do not deploy from a push on this project (memory: prove a\n430:  function deploy with the build marker), so it is its own change; the\n431:  client flag removes every launch point in the product and that is what\n432:  Joshua asked for.\n433:- `TutorChatModal`, token budgeting.\n434:- Sealed runs, practice-test sittings, full-section mode, the classic\n435:  `DrillTopBar` layout: behaviour preserved.\n436:- Reading Comprehension (`RcChoiceDisclosure`) and the Classroom\n437:  `LRQuestionModal`.\n438:- Video explanations: no toggle, no hosting, no claim.\n439:- Authoring the missing explanations.\n'}]

(launch.minutes ? ## assistant

The revision resolves most prior findings, but four blockers remain:

1. **`actionInFlight` is not a real lock.** A `useState<boolean>` update is asynchronous, so two clicks before rerender both see `false` and enter `commitThenNavigate`.
   Fix: Acquire a synchronous `useRef<boolean>` lock before the first `await`, with state used only to rerender disabled controls.

2. **Freeze-on-reveal is lost on refresh.** `revealedEverByQid` is explicitly memory-only, while Run Record snapshots omit `answerRevealed` (`Drill.tsx:868-887`); after remount, a previously revealed fixed-list answer becomes editable recorded work again.
   Fix: Persist an `answerRevealed`/frozen marker in the Run Record and reconstruct `revealedEverByQid` from it.

3. **Practice cross-outs still mutate recorded work.** `closeOpenVisit` copies `eliminatedRef` into `session.attempts` (`Drill.tsx:1254-1278`), so cross-outs made after freezing reach the attempt and resume snapshot despite the plan promising all frozen interactions are practice-only.
   Fix: Store frozen eliminations separately and make `closeOpenVisit` ignore practice-state eliminations.

4. **`questions` is not reliably a `fixed-list`.** `drillRunSpecFromLaunch` allows a `questions` launch to carry `natural-drill`; that initialization branch does not set `isPracticeSetMode`, so picks do not enter `session.attempts` immediately as the plan’s fixed-list policy assumes.
   Fix: Derive pick semantics independently from `isPracticeSetMode`, or classify `questions` from its resolved mode/config instead of unconditionally as `fixed-list`.

5. **`effectiveAnswer` breaks unfrozen fixed-commit revisits.** Its precedence is `practice → session attempt → selectedAnswer`; after returning to a committed-but-unrevealed question, a new selection written to `selectedAnswer` is masked by the existing attempt even though the plan says pre-freeze behavior remains unchanged.
   Fix: Either freeze fixed-commit questions upon commit or make the accessor/writer explicitly support visible pre-reveal revisions and define whether they can replace the persisted attempt.

6. **The End wrapper has no defined success result for fixed-list questions.** `commitPendingSelection` currently returns nothing when the question already has an attempt, while `commitThenNavigate` is specified to move only on success and only describes the returned attempts map for `fixed-commit`.
   Fix: Return a discriminated result such as `{ ok: true, attempts } | { ok: false }` for every no-op, fixed-list, unscored, and persisted path.

7. **The proposed failure event cannot supply `errorKind`.** `persistAttempt` catches the underlying exception and returns only `boolean` (`Drill.tsx:1512-1524`), discarding the information `reportCommitFailure` claims to report.
   Fix: Return a typed result carrying the failure category, or report the structured event inside the catch where the error still exists.

VERDICT: REVISE
